# Worklog — Landmark Inter Gulf: Workforce, Payroll & Accounts System

> Single shared worklog for all agents. READ before starting, APPEND after finishing (never overwrite).
> App is fully specified by `upload/GLM Build Prompt Workforce Payroll System.md`, adapted to **Next.js 16 + TypeScript + Prisma/SQLite + shadcn/ui** (env constraint: no Laravel).

---
Task ID: 1
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: Foundation — schema, seed, shared libs, app shell, stubs

Work Log: (in progress — this section documents the architecture contracts ALL agents must follow)

## PROJECT OVERVIEW
Web admin system for a construction manpower supply company (Saudi Arabia, SAR currency). Brand: "Landmark Inter Gulf" (configurable in Settings). Modules: Dashboard, Projects, Staff (list/categories/attendance/payroll/salary sheet/salary summary), Account Research (income/expense/summary/categories), Roles, Users, Settings.

## TECH (FIXED)
- Next.js 16 App Router, TypeScript, Tailwind CSS 4, shadcn/ui (New York), lucide-react icons, recharts, zustand, sonner toasts.
- ONLY user-visible page route: `/` (src/app/page.tsx). The whole app is an SPA with client-side view switching. All backend logic lives in API route handlers under `src/app/api/**`.
- Database: Prisma + SQLite (`db/custom.db`). `import { db } from '@/lib/db'`.
- NO `bun run build`. Dev server runs on port 3000 (`bun run dev`, already running in background).

## DATABASE SCHEMA (prisma/schema.prisma — Task 1, done)
Models: User, Session, Role, Setting, Project, StaffCategory, Staff (soft delete), ProjectStaff (pivot), Attendance, Payroll (unique [staffId, projectId, month, year] — but code-level duplicate check needed since SQLite treats NULLs as distinct), Transaction, TransactionCategory, ActivityLog.
Money = Float, rounded to 2dp via helpers. Dates = DateTime (ISO in JSON).

## SHARED LIBS (Task 1, done — DO NOT duplicate)
- `src/lib/db.ts` — prisma client (exists)
- `src/lib/types.ts` — all shared TS types (Staff, Project, Payroll, Transaction, Role, User, Settings, list responses)
- `src/lib/format.ts` — formatMoney(n) → "SAR 13,390.00", formatDate, formatDateTime, formatDuration(minutes) → "2w 1d 8h 30m" style, monthName
- `src/lib/payroll.ts` — `calcPayroll(input)` PURE function shared by client (live form calc) AND server (re-calc, never trust client). Formula: regular=hours×rate; ot=otHours×otRate; gross=regular+ot+otherEarnings; otherDed=(type==='percent')?gross×value/100:value; totalDed=advance+absentPenalty+pt+unionFees+otherDed; net=max(0,gross−totalDed) (+negativeWarning). VERIFICATION EXAMPLE (must match exactly): 200h × 75 = 15,000 + other 100 = gross 15,100; union 200; other ded 10% = 1,510; total ded 1,710 → **net = SAR 13,390.00**
- `src/lib/auth.ts` — server-only: scrypt hash/verify password, createSession/getSessionUser( cookies() ), requireAuth(req) helper
- `src/lib/api-helpers.ts` — server-only: ok(data), badRequest(error), notFound(), unauthorized(), forbidden(), serverError(); parsePagination(searchParams); requirePermission(user, perm) — "*" wildcard support; logActivity(...)
- `src/lib/api-client.ts` — client fetch wrapper `api.get/post/put/del`
- `src/lib/store.ts` — zustand store: { user, settings, view, setView, logout } (persist view not needed)

## AUTH MODEL
- Cookie session: HttpOnly cookie `lig_session` = token, stored in Session table (30d expiry).
- Login: admin@example.com / password (seeded Super Admin, permissions ["*"]).
- Every API route (except /api/auth/login, /api/auth/me) must call requireAuth() → 401 if no session. Write endpoints additionally check permission → 403.
- Permission strings: `dashboard.view`, `projects.view|create|edit|delete`, `staff.*`, `attendance.*`, `payroll.*` + `payroll.print`, `accounts.*`, `roles.*`, `users.*`, `settings.view|edit`.

## API CONTRACT (all agents MUST implement/consume exactly)
All responses JSON. Lists: `{ data, page, pageSize, total, totalPages }`. Errors: `{ error: "message" }` with proper status (400/401/403/404/409/500).
- POST /api/auth/login {email,password} → {user} + sets cookie | POST /api/auth/logout | GET /api/auth/me → {user}|401
- GET /api/dashboard → { stats:{totalStaff,totalUsers,totalIncome,totalExpense,netBalance,salaryPaid}, chart:[{month,income,expense}] (last 6 months), recentActivity:[{id,description,amount?,createdAt,userName?}] }
- GET /api/projects?search&status&page&pageSize → data rows: {id,name,managerName,location,status,createdAt,staffCount}
- POST /api/projects {name,managerName,location,status}; GET/PUT /api/projects/[id]; DELETE → 409 `{error:"...assigned staff..."}` if project has staff/payrolls/attendances
- GET /api/staff-categories → {data:[{id,name,description,staffCount}]}; POST/PUT/DELETE (409 if staff assigned)
- GET /api/staff?search&projectId&categoryId&status&page&pageSize → rows include {id,fullName,phone,position,iqamaId,joiningDate,ratePerHour,overtimeRate,monthlySalary,profilePhoto,status, staffCategory:{id,name}, projects:[{id,name}]}
- POST /api/staff {fullName,phone,position,staffCategoryId,iqamaId,joiningDate,ratePerHour,overtimeRate,monthlySalary,profilePhoto?,residentialAddress?,status,projectIds:[]}; GET/PUT/DELETE (soft delete)
- GET /api/attendance?staffId&projectId&from&to&search&page&pageSize → rows {id,staff:{id,fullName,iqamaId},project:{id,name}|null,checkIn,checkOut,durationMinutes}
- POST /api/attendance {staffId,projectId?,checkIn,checkOut?}; PUT/DELETE /api/attendance/[id]
- GET /api/attendance/hours?staffId&month&year → {totalMinutes,totalHours}
- GET /api/payrolls?search&projectId&categoryId&month&year&status&page&pageSize → rows {id,month,year,totalHours,hourlyRateSnapshot,overtimeHours,overtimeRateSnapshot,otherEarnings,advance,absentPenalty,ptAmount,unionFees,otherDeductionValue,otherDeductionType,grossPay,totalDeductions,netPay,status,payDate,payType,taxCode,staff:{id,fullName,iqamaId,staffCategory:{name}},project:{id,name}|null} + `totals:{gross,deductions,net}` for current filter
- POST /api/payrolls {staffId,projectId?,month,year,totalHours,hourlyRate,overtimeHours,overtimeRate,otherEarnings,advance,absentPenalty,ptAmount,unionFees,otherDeductionValue,otherDeductionType,status,payDate,payType,taxCode} → server recalcs everything via calcPayroll; duplicate check (staff+project+month+year) → 409 friendly error; if status=paid → create linked expense Transaction (category "Salary", title `Salary Payment: NAME`, amount=netPay, payrollId) in $transaction; wrap in prisma.$transaction
- GET/PUT /api/payrolls/[id] (PUT: recalc + sync linked transaction: delete if back to pending, update amount if still paid); DELETE → also delete linked expense transaction
- GET /api/payrolls/sheet?projectId&categoryId&month&year&search → { rows:[sheet rows], totals:{count,gross,deductions,net}, breakdown:[{categoryId,name,manpower,totalHours,grossPay,deductions,totalAmount}] }
- GET /api/payrolls/summary?projectId&month&year → { cards:{totalManpower,totalGross,totalDeductions,totalDisbursement}, rows:[{name,manpower,totalHours,otHours,grossPay,deductions,totalAmount,share}] sorted totalAmount desc, zero-rows last }
- GET /api/transactions?type=income|expense&categoryId&projectId&from&to&search&page&pageSize → rows {id,type,title,amount,transactionDate,description,category:{id,name,type},project:{id,name}|null,operator:{id,name}|null,payrollId} + totals:{sum}
- POST /api/transactions {type,title,amount,transactionCategoryId,projectId?,operatorUserId,transactionDate,description?}; PUT/DELETE [id] → 409 if payrollId linked ("Managed via Payroll module")
- GET /api/transaction-categories?type= → {data:[{id,name,type,transactionCount}]}; POST/PUT/DELETE (409 if used)
- GET /api/accounts/summary?from&to&projectId → { totals:{income,expense,net}, byMonth:[{month,income,expense,net}], byCategory:[{name,type,amount}] }
- GET /api/roles → {data:[{id,name,description,permissions:string[],userCount}]}; POST/PUT/DELETE (409 if users assigned)
- GET /api/users → {data:[{id,name,email,avatar,isActive,createdAt,role:{id,name}|null}]}; POST {name,email,password,roleId?,avatar?}; PUT (password optional); DELETE (409 if self or last Super Admin)
- GET /api/settings → {settings:{companyName,address,phone,email,logo,currency,defaultPayType}}; PUT /api/settings (same shape, logo=dataURL)
- GET /api/activity?limit=20 → {data:[ActivityLog rows desc]}

## FRONTEND ARCHITECTURE (Task 1 creates shell + STUB views; Task 4 agents replace stubs)
- `src/app/page.tsx` → dynamic import of `<App/>`; `'use client'` everywhere in components/app
- `src/components/app/app.tsx` — fetches /api/auth/me + /api/settings; shows LoginScreen or AppShell; ThemeProvider(next-themes) + Sonner Toaster
- `src/components/app/login-screen.tsx` — split-screen login, brand from settings
- `src/components/app/sidebar.tsx` — dark navy (light+dark mode), collapsible submenus (Staff Management, Account Research), brand logo+name, active item blue highlight, user chip (initials avatar + name + role) at bottom, mobile Sheet drawer
- `src/components/app/topbar.tsx` — page title, dark-mode toggle (Sun/Moon), quick actions on dashboard (Add Income/Add Expense), mobile menu button
- `src/components/app/views/` — one file per view (registry in `views/registry.tsx` created by Task 1; agents ONLY replace their own view files, NEVER touch registry/shell/sidebar):
  - dashboard.tsx, projects.tsx, staff-list.tsx, staff-categories.tsx, attendance.tsx, payroll.tsx, salary-sheet.tsx, salary-summary.tsx, income-list.tsx, expense-list.tsx, accounts-summary.tsx, transaction-categories.tsx, roles.tsx, users.tsx, settings.tsx
- `src/components/app/print/salary-slip.tsx` — full-screen print dialog (A4, company header, EARNINGS/DEDUCTIONS tables, dark NET bar, Close + Print buttons)
- `src/components/app/print/salary-sheet-print.tsx` — A4 landscape print page (title "Salary Sheet — {Month Year}", totals, SN table, category breakdown, Receiver/Accountant/Manager signature blocks)
- Shared UI (Task 1, done): `src/components/app/shared/page-header.tsx`, `stat-card.tsx`, `status-badge.tsx`, `confirm-delete.tsx` (AlertDialog), `empty-state.tsx`, `data-table-pagination.tsx`, `money.tsx`, `filter-select.tsx`
- Print CSS in globals.css: `.print-area`, `@media print` rules, A4 page setup.
- State: zustand store holds {user, settings, view}; setView('payroll') switches views. Data fetching: plain fetch via api-client + useEffect/useCallback (TanStack optional). Toasts: sonner. Confirm before delete: ConfirmDelete component. Search box + filter dropdowns + pagination (10/25/50) on ALL lists.

## STYLE RULES (MANDATORY for all agents)
- Colors: professional blue primary (oklch ~0.55 0.19 255), navy sidebar `#0B1B33`-ish in both modes. Status badges: Active=blue, Pending=amber, Paid=green, Inactive=gray, Completed=green, Income=green, Expense=red.
- Cards: rounded-xl, soft shadow (`shadow-sm`), consistent padding `p-4`/`p-6`, gaps `gap-4`/`gap-6`.
- Money: ALWAYS `formatMoney()` → "SAR 13,390.00" (tabular-nums).
- Responsive: mobile-first; tables → horizontal scroll on mobile (`overflow-x-auto`); sidebar → drawer; filter bars stack.
- Buttons: shadcn Button; destructive for delete; icons from lucide-react; 44px touch targets on mobile.
- Dark mode via next-themes (`dark` class). All custom colors must have dark: variants.
- Lists: max-h with overflow-y-auto + custom scrollbar for very long lists.
- NO blue-600 indigo-800 overload: use primary tokens. No emojis in UI.

## SEED DATA (prisma/seed.ts, Task 1)
Admin user (admin@example.com/password, Super Admin) + 3 extra users (Accountant/HR/Viewer), 4 roles, 6 projects, 13 staff categories (PIPING QCI, PIPING QCS, PIPE FITTER, PIPING WELDER, WELDING QCS, RIGGER, HELPER, LINE CHECKER, COATING QCI, COORDINATOR, OFFICE MANAGER, PICKUP DRIVER, PLATE WELDER), 40 staff w/ Iqama IDs + varied rates, attendance last 2 months (~15 records/staff), payrolls for previous month (~30, mixed pending/paid, paid ones have linked expense transactions), income/expense rows (last 6 months), settings row, activity logs. Seed is idempotent (checks existing counts).

'bank_transfer'}}; PUT (settings.edit; upserts each provided key; logo:null clears row; defaultPayType enum-validated; logActivity 'settings'; returns same shape)

Task ID: 2-a
Agent: full-stack-developer
Task: Backend API — auth, projects, staff, staff-categories, attendance

Work Log:
- Read worklog + shared libs (api-helpers, auth, types, format) + prisma schema; verified zod v4.3.5 and Prisma 6.19.2 runtime behaviors before coding (z.preprocess/refine/enum-error patterns; createMany on SQLite)
- Found & verified: `createMany({ skipDuplicates })` THROWS `PrismaClientValidationError` on SQLite ("Unknown argument skipDuplicates") → deduped projectIds in code (`Array.from(new Set(...))`) + deleteMany→createMany sync instead of skipDuplicates
- Auth: POST /api/auth/login (zod-validated, isActive check, scrypt verify, HttpOnly `lig_session` cookie 30d lax, logs 'login' activity), POST /api/auth/logout (destroySession + cookie maxAge 0, no auth), GET /api/auth/me (ALWAYS 200, {user|null})
- Projects: GET list (search/status filters, createdAt desc, _count staff, paginated ProjectRow), POST (projects.create, zod friendly errors), [id] GET (project + staff[] with category+projects via pivot include), PUT (projects.edit), DELETE (projects.delete → 409 if staff/payrolls/attendances/transactions exist)
- Staff categories: GET all (name asc, staffCount via `db.staff.groupBy({by:['staffCategoryId'], where:{deletedAt:null}, _count:{_all:true}})` merged in a Map — no N+1), POST/PUT/DELETE with case-insensitive duplicate name check (409) and in-use guard (`Cannot delete: category is assigned to N staff members...`)
- Staff: GET list (always deletedAt:null; search fullName OR iqamaId; projectId via `projects:{some:{projectId}}`; categoryId; status; fullName asc; include category + projects), POST (staff.create; category existence, IQAMA unique incl. soft-deleted holders + P2002 safety net → 409 friendly, photo dataURL prefix + <3MB check, projects existence, joiningDate via parseDate), [id] GET (404 if deleted), PUT (staff.edit; project sync in $transaction — deleteMany+createMany BEFORE update so returned row is fresh), DELETE = soft delete (deletedAt, `Removed staff: NAME`)
- Attendance: GET list (staffId/projectId/search-via-staff/from/to on checkIn, checkIn desc, include staff+project), POST (attendance.create; staff exists & not deleted; checkOut strictly after checkIn; server-computed durationMinutes), [id] PUT (recompute duration) + DELETE, GET /api/attendance/hours (staffId+month+year required, aggregate _sum durationMinutes over [1st, next 1st), round2 hours)
- Tested everything via curl (59 checks): login (bad creds 401, bad body 400, success 200 + cookie), me (with/without cookie, after logout), 401 on protected routes, full CRUD + all 409/404/400 guards, filters, pagination, hours math (630min→10.5h; after PUT 450min→7.5h), soft-delete visibility, logout clears cookie
- Cleaned ALL test data afterwards (test staff/projects/categories/attendance + 29 test activity logs) — DB back to seeded state (6 projects, 40 staff, 13 categories, 1346 attendance)
- eslint clean + tsc clean on all my files; dev.log has no unexpected errors

Stage Summary:
- Files created (12): src/app/api/auth/{login,logout,me}/route.ts, src/app/api/projects/route.ts + [id]/route.ts, src/app/api/staff-categories/route.ts + [id]/route.ts, src/app/api/staff/route.ts + [id]/route.ts, src/app/api/attendance/route.ts + [id]/route.ts + hours/route.ts
- All 59 curl checks passed; DB left pristine
- DEVIATIONS/notes for other agents:
  1. **skipDuplicates is NOT supported on SQLite** (PrismaClientValidationError) — anyone using createMany must dedupe in code. Relevant to Task 2-b if bulk-creating.
  2. Attendance `to` filter is inclusive end-of-day (23:59:59.999) so yyyy-mm-dd ranges cover the full last day (slight deviation from "checkIn lte to" literal reading; matches UX intent).
  3. Login logs an activity row (action 'login', module 'user') — shows up in dashboard recent activity.
  4. IQAMA uniqueness is checked across ALL staff including soft-deleted (DB unique constraint spans them; friendly 409 instead of 500).
  5. Create endpoints return the created row flat (ProjectRow/StaffRow/AttendanceRow shape, 201); update endpoints return the updated row flat (200); deletes return {success:true}.
  6. Staff-category duplicate-name check is case-insensitive.
  7. No shared lib changes needed — everything was implementable as-is.

Login verified via API (200 + Super Admin user) AND agent-browser UI E2E (fill form → submit → dashboard renders with data; VLM: "fully populated, no blank areas").

- FEATURE A (major, prevents the exact bug the user hit) — Self-healing bootstrap:
  * REFACTOR: extracted ALL seed logic from prisma/seed.ts into NEW `src/lib/seed-data.ts` exporting `runSeed(db): Promise<SeedSummary>` (deterministic RNG moved inside the function; returns counts). prisma/seed.ts is now a thin CLI wrapper importing it (verified: runs, richer summary output incl. attendance/transaction counts).
  * NEW `src/app/api/bootstrap/route.ts`: GET → `{needsBootstrap, userCount}` (public — only reveals whether DB is empty); POST → runs runSeed ONLY while userCount === 0, else 409 "Database is already initialized" (can never wipe a live system).
  * login-screen.tsx: on mount checks GET /api/bootstrap; empty DB → dedicated amber "Database is empty" card (DatabaseZap icon, feature list, Initialize button) → initializing spinner state → seeds → pre-fills admin credentials + toast; settings refetched after init so branding renders.
  * VERIFIED E2E (the full disaster-recovery path): wiped DB via temp script (same FK-safe order as seed wipe) → reloaded page → bootstrap card rendered → clicked Initialize → demo data re-created → login form returned pre-filled → signed in. Also verified GET (needsBootstrap:false) and POST (409) on a populated DB.
- FEATURE B — Change password (auth hardening):
  * NEW `src/app/api/auth/change-password/route.ts`: requireAuth + zod (new pw 8-100 chars); verifies current password (400 "Current password is incorrect"), rejects same-as-current; scrypt-hash update; logs activity "changed own password"; session stays valid.
  * NEW `src/components/app/change-password-dialog.tsx`: 3 fields with per-field show/hide toggles, live strength meter (Weak/Fair/Strong — 3-segment bar, rose/amber/emerald), mismatch validation, disabled submit, error alert, success toast; form resets on open.
  * sidebar.tsx user dropdown: + "Change password" item (KeyRound icon) above Log out.
  * VERIFIED E2E: wrong current pw → inline error; correct → toast + dialog close → logout → sign-in with NEW password works → changed back to `password` (DB hash verify: true; 2 audit rows in Activity Log).
- FEATURE C — Login screen polish (styling mandate):
  * Password show/hide: icon toggle inside input + "Show/Hide" text button in label row.
  * Caps Lock warning: amber alert under password field (getModifierState; verified via overridden synthetic event — CDP `press` doesn't emulate CapsLock modifier, but handler wiring proven).
  * Demo credential quick-fill chips: Admin/Accountant/HR/Viewer 2-col grid, one click fills email+password, active chip highlighted, hint text.
  * Error feedback: framer-motion shake (x keyframes on re-trigger via key), role=alert + aria-live=assertive.

ALWAYS use a named session. Also: Radix DropdownMenu needs pointerdown+pointerup events (synthetic .click() alone doesn't open it); direct `el.value=''` desyncs React controlled inputs — use fill().

Task ID: R17 (user-requested feature)
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: Round 17 — USER REQUEST: new "Advance for Workers" module — advance entry (worker, amount, date, method, notes, deduction month, installments), list/reports (filters, monthly summary, per-worker ledger, edit/delete locks, PDF/Excel export), automatic payroll integration (month+year matching, never-negative capping with carry-over, mark deducted on finalize, revert on reopen/delete, stale warning + recalculate), accounting (expense on payout, income on recovery), permissions (Admin/Accountant full, HR add+view, Viewer view, audit log), dashboard card, worker-profile Advance tab, mobile-responsive UI.

Work Log:
- SCHEMA (prisma/schema.prisma): new WorkerAdvance model (staffId, amount, givenDate, paymentMethod cash|bank_transfer, reference, deductionMonth/Year, installments, deductedAmount, status, transactionId link, createdById) + PayrollAdvanceDeduction link model (payrollId, advanceId, amount, recoveryTransactionId — @@unique([payrollId, advanceId])). Relations added to Staff/Payroll/Transaction. db:push OK (dev server restart REQUIRED after push — stale Prisma client caused 500s until restart; known ops note).
- PURE SCHEDULING (src/lib/advances.ts, shared client+server): scheduledForAdvance(adv, month, year) — installment plan with cumulative target = min(amount, share × min(monthsElapsed, installments)); before first deduction month → 0 (month AND year must match: Oct 2026 ≠ Oct 2025); past the window the remaining balance keeps carrying until fully recovered (handles under-deduction catch-up). capAdvanceDeductions(advances, room) — oldest-first allocation capped to salary room (net never negative). advanceStatusOf (pending | partially_deducted | deducted), monthlyInstallment, advanceRef (ADV-XXXXXX), lastInstallmentMonth.
- SERVER ENGINE (src/lib/advance-service.ts): syncPayrollAdvanceDeductions (revert old rows → month-aware recompute → cap → write rows → bump balances → recovery income entries when paid), revertPayrollAdvanceDeductions (payroll delete), deductedUpToMonth (MONTH-AWARE: only payrolls ≤ target month count — fixes past-month edit after later months deducted), expectedAdvanceForPayroll (stale simulation), ensureRecoveryTransactions (idempotent, used by sync + mark-paid), isAdvanceLocked (paid-payroll reference ⇒ 409), serializeAdvance.
- APIs (7 new routes): /api/advances GET (search name/IQAMA/reference OR-combined, staffId, month, year, status, project filters + totals given/deducted/outstanding + locked flags) + POST (validates, creates linked "Advance to Workers" expense); /api/advances/[id] PUT (409 when locked; syncs linked expense; worker immutable) + DELETE (409 when locked; removes expense; AUTO-RESYNCS affected pending payrolls so deletion never leaves stale columns); /api/advances/summary (cards + last-12-months given totals); /api/advances/ledger?staffId (advances + payroll deduction timeline + totals + locked); /api/advances/payroll-preview?staffId&month&year&excludePayrollId (form auto-fill, month-aware); /api/advances/stale-payrolls?month&year (expected > 0 guard — legacy manual advance values are never flagged/touched); /api/advances/recalculate POST {month, year} (same guard; updates advance/totalDeductions/netPay + linked salary expense).
- PAYROLL INTEGRATION: POST/PUT /api/payrolls create payroll with placeholder advance → sync → matching advances REPLACE the manual value (no matches ⇒ manual value kept) → final calc + salary transaction sync; response gains advanceApplied {count,total} + capped-carry warning. DELETE reverts advances + removes recovery entries. mark-paid endpoint now calls ensureRecoveryTransactions (BUG FOUND IN QA: recovery entries were missing when finalizing via the quick action — fixed + verified).
- UI — ADVANCES VIEW (new advances.tsx): PageHeader + Print PDF (A4 landscape, print-only heading + table) + Excel/CSV export (13 columns, filtered); 4 StatCards (Outstanding amber / Total Advanced / Recovered / Workers with Advances); Monthly Summary side panel (12-month bars); FilterBar (search + month + year + status + project); desktop table (min-w-760 + h-scroll, ref/worker/amount/given+method/deduction+installments/deducted/remaining/status/ledger-edit-delete actions, locked rows show Lock icon + disabled) + mobile cards + pagination; AdvanceFormDialog (worker searchable combobox, amount, date given, method, notes, deduction month/year defaults from advance date until touched, installments switch + months, live deduction-plan preview); AdvanceLedgerDialog + reusable AdvanceLedgerContent (totals + advances table + payroll deduction timeline with recovered state); MobileFab; command-palette deep-open create intent.
- UI — PAYROLL INTEGRATION (payroll.tsx): form fetches payroll-preview on staff+period change; Advance Deduction field LOCKED with amber breakdown (ref, amount, installment n/N, capped-carry note, "managed in Worker Advances"); effectiveAdvance = min(preview total, room) replaces manual value; CalcRow + CSV column renamed "Advance Deduction"; list view gains amber stale banner (staff names + Recalculate Payroll button, payroll.edit gated) auto-refreshing with filters.
- UI — STEP 3: dashboard API + card "Total Advance Outstanding" (advances.view gated, links to view); staff profile dialog now tabbed Profile/Advances (Tabs UI, advance ledger embedded); salary slip line "Staff Advance" → "Advance Deduction"; salary sheet column "Advance" → "Advance Deduction"; payroll form labels renamed.
- PERMISSIONS: new 'advances' module (view/create/edit/delete) in permissions.ts catalog (Roles UI picks it up automatically); DB roles updated — Accountant +full, HR Manager +view+create, Viewer +view (Super Admin has *). Activity log: 'advance' module label + HandCoins icon.
- QA (curl, 20+ test cases on Alamin Hossain, ALL PASSED, data cleaned): same-month match (Oct advance → Oct payroll deducts 1000, net 1500); different-month/year no-match (Sep 2026, Oct 2025 → 0); over-salary capping (5000 advance vs 2500 salary → deduct 2500, warning, net 0, carry 2500 → Nov deducts remainder); installments (3000 × 3 → exactly 1000/1000/1000 then 0); multi-advance allocation oldest-first with catch-up; past-month edit after later deductions (month-aware fix verified: Dec edit keeps its 1000); paid payroll locks advance (409 on edit/delete), reopen releases lock + removes recovery income; mark-paid creates "Advance Recovery" income; delete payroll reverts advance to pending; stale detection (stored 400 vs expected 600 after edit) + recalculate fixes + banner clears; viewer 403 on POST, HR 400-past-permission on POST + 403 on DELETE; search filters (name/reference/IQAMA/status/month).
- QA (agent-browser E2E, 2 sessions): login → sidebar nav → Give Advance dialog (worker combobox search, amount, installments toggle, live "SAR 400.00 × 3 installments starting October 2026" plan) → row + 4 stat cards + monthly bar → payroll create with locked Advance Deduction field showing "Auto-applied from ADV-… (installment 1/3)" + toasts → advance edit 1200→1800 → stale banner in payroll view → Recalculate updates 400→600 net 1900 → salary slip shows "Advance Deduction" → staff profile Advances tab (totals 1800/600/1200, timeline "Finalized — advance recovered") → ledger dialog → locked icon after mark-paid → mobile 390px (FIXED horizontal overflow: implicit grid auto column sized to max-content — added grid-cols-1; verified sw=390) → dark mode. VLM review: "high-quality UI implementation" — fixed stat-card label tooltip + table min-w so headers never truncate (DOM-verified all 9 headers trunc:false).
- BUGS FOUND & FIXED DURING QA: (1) stale Prisma client after db:push — dev server restart required; (2) past-month payroll edit lost its installment (cumulative target saw future months' deductions) — month-aware deductedUpToMonth; (3) mark-paid quick action skipped recovery income entries — ensureRecoveryTransactions wired in; (4) advances search AND-ed staff-match with reference-match — proper OR; (5) stale detector flagged ALL legacy manual-advance payrolls (would zero them on recalc) — expected > 0 guard; (6) advance deletion left stale pending payrolls — auto-resync in DELETE handler; (7) mobile grid overflow — grid-cols-1.
- FINAL: lint 0 errors; tsc 0 app errors; dev.log clean; all test data (payrolls, advances, transactions, activity rows) removed — user's data untouched (Alamin's real Sep payroll intact).

Stage Summary:
- Round 17 complete: full "Advance for Workers" module delivered in the requested 3 steps — entry/list/ledger UI, automatic payroll integration (month+year matching, never-negative capping with carry-over, finalize-lock/reopen-revert, stale warning + one-click recalculate), accounting double-entry (Advance to Workers expense ↔ Advance Recovery income), role permissions + audit log, dashboard outstanding card, worker-profile Advance tab, PDF/Excel export, mobile-responsive. 23 files touched (11 new), all 4 requested test-case categories verified end-to-end plus 7 additional bugs caught and fixed during QA.
- Design decisions worth remembering: payroll.advance is authoritative-from-module whenever ANY matching advance exists (manual fallback only when none match); deduction rows update balances on generation (pending) but the EDIT/DELETE lock applies only after a PAID payroll references the advance; carry-over continues past the installment window until the balance is fully recovered (by design, per "carry the remaining balance to the next month").
- Known non-blocking items (carried): backup import/restore; expiry window as setting; LICENSE file; toStaffRow dedup.

Task ID: 20
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: Client Salary Summary — hours MIRROR PAYROLL + manual per-worker client rate input (client rate ≠ worker pay rate)

User request (Bengali): "Client Salary Summary te hours and rate input kora jacce na. Same month a payroll and client salary summary same name workers working hours same thakbe kintu rate manually input kore nebo karon dui jaygay rate different thakbe. Client rate and workers pay rate alada."
→ (1) Hours could not be input/were wrong — they must be EXACTLY the payroll hours for the same month. (2) The client rate must be entered MANUALLY per worker (it is a separate price from the worker's pay rate).

Work Log:
- Root cause: the summary sourced hours from raw ATTENDENCE aggregation (7,050 h) while payroll held 6,310 h + 529 OT for Sept 2026 — numbers could never match, and rates only existed as a project-level setting (no per-worker input).
- Schema: added `Payroll.clientRate Float @default(0)` + `Payroll.clientOtRate Float @default(0)` (manually-entered CLIENT billing rates stored ON the payroll row — snapshot semantics like hourlyRateSnapshot; 0 = inherit). `bun run db:push` + dev-server restart (known gotcha). Verified the suspicious `@@index(onth, year])` seen in grep output was a display artifact — od showed clean bytes `@@index([month, year])`.
- Payroll APIs: POST /api/payrolls now prefills `clientRate` from `project.clientRatePerHour` when the caller omits it (project lookup extended); PUT /api/payrolls/[id] PRESERVES existing clientRate/clientOtRate when the payroll form omits them (`?? existing`). Both schemas gained optional 0–100000 fields.
- NEW `PUT /api/payrolls/[id]/client-rate`: lightweight editor {clientRate?, clientOtRate?}, perm payroll.edit, activity-logged — never touches pay rates.
- NEW `PUT /api/clients/salary-summary/rates`: bulk apply {month, year, projectId, clientRate, clientOtRate?, onlyWithHours=true} → updateMany on the project's payroll rows; activity-logged.
- Types: ClientBillingLine gained payrollId, otHours, clientOtRate, manualRate/manualOtRate (raw stored), projectRate (fallback), rateSource now 'manual' | 'project' | 'worker'; ClientSalaryGroup + cards gained otHours/billedOtHours; PayrollRow gained clientRate/clientOtRate.
- GET /api/clients/salary-summary REWRITTEN: source = Payroll records for month+year (staff not deleted, active by default). hours = totalHours, otHours = overtimeHours (EXACT salary-sheet numbers). Rate resolution: payroll.clientRate > 0 → manual; else project.clientRatePerHour → project; else worker's hourlyRateSnapshot → worker (⚠). OT effective = clientOtRate > 0 ? clientOtRate : effective regular. amount = h×rate + ot×otRate; workerCost = h×workerRate + ot×workerOtRate (payroll snapshots). Groups/cards/sorting/search all preserved; "No Client (Unlinked Payroll)" bucket last; workersWithHours counts h+OT > 0.
- View client-salary-summary.tsx REWRITTEN:
  - RateCell: always-editable inline number input per worker (prefilled with the EFFECTIVE rate) — live amount recompute while typing (edits overlay + recomputeSummary pure function rebuilds rows/subtotals/VAT/grand/share/cards), commits on Enter/blur via the client-rate endpoint, Esc reverts, invalid input toasts + resets, saved → green ✓ 1.4s, focus+blur without typing does NOT save (dirty flag), syncs from parent (bulk apply/reload) while not editing via render-time prop adjustment (React lint-safe: no setState-in-effect, no ref reads in render).
  - OT Rate input shown per row when the worker has OT hours (or a manual OT rate) with "= regular"/"manual" hint.
  - BulkRateApply per project bucket: "Apply to all N: [rate] [OT optional] Apply" → bulk endpoint → local overlay for every row (rows with own manual OT keep it).
  - Hours/OT cells carry a tiny Lock icon + titles "Mirrors this month's payroll — edit hours in the Payroll module"; table headers tooltip the same.

Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: User request (Bengali): "Expense List ke emn vabe banaw ze expense korce tar name and iqama number thakbe and month. bill recept uplode kora zabe. ke koto taka expense korece tar every month statement print kora zabe ba total dekha zabe" — rebuild the Expense List with the spender's name + IQAMA + month on every entry, bill receipt upload, and per-worker monthly totals printable as a statement.

Work Log:
- Schema (prisma/schema.prisma): Transaction += spenderStaffId/spender (Staff relation "ExpenseSpender", SetNull), expenseMonth/expenseYear (month the expense BELONGS to), receiptExt/receiptName/receiptSize (bill receipt metadata) + indexes [spenderStaffId], [expenseYear, expenseMonth]. Staff += expenses back-relation. `bun run db:push` + dev-server restart (known gotcha).
- Backfill (scripts/backfill-expense-month.ts, bun): derived expenseMonth/expenseYear from transactionDate for all 47 pre-existing expense rows so old data appears in month filters/statements.
- Types: TransactionRow += spender {id, fullName, iqamaId} | null, expenseMonth/expenseYear, receiptName/receiptExt/receiptSize; NEW ExpenseStatement / ExpenseStatementSpender / ExpenseStatementMonth (with per-month receipts count).
- API /api/transactions (GET/POST) + /api/transactions/[id] (GET/PUT/DELETE):
  - txInclude += spender; mapTransaction serializes the new fields.
  - GET filters += spenderId (id | 'none' → unassigned/company), expenseMonth, expenseYear; search now also matches spender fullName/IQAMA.
  - POST/PUT schema += spenderStaffId/expenseMonth/expenseYear (nullable optional); validation checks the staff exists (not deleted); month/year default from transactionDate when omitted; PUT preserves receipt* columns (owned by the receipt endpoint).
  - DELETE now also removes the receipt file from disk (uploads/receipts/{id}.{ext}).
- NEW /api/transactions/[id]/receipt (GET stream / POST upload / DELETE remove): files stored on disk under <project>/uploads/receipts, served ONLY through the authenticated GET (Content-Type + inline disposition, cookies ride along for <img>/iframe); upload = multipart FormData, JPG/PNG/WebP/PDF only, ≤ 5 MB, replace removes the old file first; payroll-linked rows rejected (409); activity-logged.
- NEW /api/transactions/expense-statement?year[&spenderId]: groups expenses by spender × month (null month/year falls back to transactionDate), returns spenders sorted by total + unassigned bucket + grandTotal; per-month count/total/receipts.
- Frontend — expense-list.tsx REBUILT as a standalone view (income-list.tsx / TransactionsListView untouched → zero income regression):
  - Filters: All Workers (+'Company (No Worker)'), Month, Year, Category, Project + search (also matches worker name/IQAMA); CSV export includes Spender/IQAMA/Expense Month/Receipt columns.
  - Form: "Spent By (Worker)" searchable combobox (name + IQAMA + position, "Company expense (no worker)" option, rose IQAMA badge), Expense Month/Year selects that auto-follow the DATE until manually changed (touch refs reset per dialog), Bill Receipt file input (create uploads after save; edit shows the attached file with View/Replace/Remove).

- NEW print component expense-statement-print.tsx: single worker → A4 PORTRAIT letterhead statement (worker identity, month rows with entries/receipts/total, YEAR TOTAL, amount in words, Prepared/Approved/Received-By signatures); all workers → A4 LANDSCAPE matrix (workers × months + Company row + grand total). Uses usePageRule like the other prints.

- QA (agent-browser, Super Admin):

  - Created "Safety boots purchase — Abdul Kareem" SAR 350.50, worker Abdul Kareem (IQAMA 2471889915, badge verified), month SEPTEMBER while date = Oct 8 (independent month proven), receipt test-bill-receipt.png (53 KB) uploaded on save → paperclip button, preview dialog <img> naturalWidth 720 (file served through the authed API), Replace/Remove present.

Received By the worker) or everyone (landscape matrix).

(3) ok() returns the payload UNWRAPPED (no {data} envelope) — top-level fields. (4) db:push requires a dev-server restart.

- SETTINGS: new `vatRate` Setting (stored as percent string "15", GET returns AppSettings.vatRate as percent, PUT accepts 0–100). Wired into: settings API (buildSettings + schema + clampVatPercent), store.ts DEFAULT_SETTINGS (15), Settings view ("VAT Rate (%)" input with formula hint), bootstrap defaults, seed-data.

- API (3 new route files):
  - GET/POST /api/vat-bills — list with search (bill no/supplier/VAT no/description) + month/year/quarter/date-range filters (precedence: date-range > quarter+year > month+year > year), pagination + totals {count, withVat, withoutVat, vat}; POST validates uniqueness (friendly 409 "already exists (dated …)"), computes the split SERVER-SIDE from the current rate (splitVat: without = round2(with/(1+r)), vat = round2(with−without)).
  - GET/PUT/DELETE /api/vat-bills/[id] — PUT re-checks uniqueness excluding self + recomputes the split; DELETE permanent.
  - GET /api/vat-bills/summary?month&year — vatRate fraction, distinct years, monthTotals, 12 monthly lines (always all 12), yearTotals, 4 quarter lines (each with its 3 month rows + grand total), currentQuarter/currentYear, quarterEndDate + ZATCA-style filingDeadline (last day of the month FOLLOWING quarter end).
- SHARED LIB: src/lib/vat.ts (server-only: getVatRate() reads the Setting, splitVat() mirrors the client calc exactly).
- VIEW (new): src/components/app/views/vat-return-expense.tsx —
  - Monthly Summary: month+year selectors + 4 StatCards (Bills count, Total With VAT, Total Without VAT, Total VAT 15%) that auto-refresh after every add/edit/delete.
  - Bills list: FilterBar (search, month, quarter, year, from→to date range with mutual-exclusion UX: quarter clears month, date-range clears both, quarter defaults year to current), desktop table + mobile stacked cards, totals chips, DataTablePagination, ConfirmDelete for delete.

- PRINT (new): src/components/app/print/vat-return-print.tsx — A4 portrait report: company letterhead (name, address, period, VAT rate, generated date), filtered bills table + TOTAL row, Quarterly VAT Breakdown (Q1–Q4 + YEAR TOTAL, selected quarter highlighted), Prepared By / Authorized Signatory signature lines. Task ID: iframe-auth-bearer-fallback
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: User report (Bengali): "Email: admin@landmarkintergulf.com Password: Landmark@2025 eta die login korco kintu ase na" + screenshot — user logs in with the correct admin credentials but the Dashboard shows "Unable to load dashboard / Authentication required".

Work Log:
- DIAGNOSIS (from screenshot via VLM + dev.log + DB): the login itself SUCCEEDED every time — the sidebar showed "landmarkintergulf / Super Admin" and the Session table had 5 rows (06:04–06:21, repeated attempts). But every follow-up GET (/api/dashboard, /api/users, /api/roles) returned 401 "Authentication required". ROOT CAUSE: the user views the app in the sandbox PREVIEW PANEL — a cross-site IFRAME. The session cookie is `SameSite=Lax; httpOnly` → browsers (Chrome/Safari) refuse to store/send Lax cookies in third-party iframe contexts → login POST returns 200 + user JSON (so the client-side store shows the user), but all subsequent API calls arrive cookie-less → 401 → "Unable to load dashboard".
- FIX — DUAL AUTH (cookie + Bearer token), cookie stays primary:
  1. src/lib/auth.ts: getSessionUser() refactored — new lookupSession(token) helper; primary path reads the lig_session cookie; FALLBACK reads `Authorization: Bearer <token>` header (via next/headers) when the cookie is absent/rejected. One choke point — every API route (requireAuth/requirePermission) benefits automatically.
  2. src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts: response JSON now includes `token` alongside `user` (the same 64-hex session token the cookie would carry).
  3. src/app/api/auth/logout/route.ts: destroys the session from cookie OR Bearer header OR {token} JSON body; still clears the cookie.
  4. src/lib/api-client.ts: new TOKEN_KEY 'lig_session_token' in localStorage + setAuthToken/getAuthToken exports; request() attaches `Authorization: Bearer` when a token exists; on any 401 the stored token is auto-cleared. (Bug fixed while rewriting: qs() ternary needed `: ''`.)
  5. login-screen.tsx: after successful login stores res.token via setAuthToken.
  6. app.tsx: handleLogout clears the token; boot() clears any STALE token when /api/auth/me returns user:null (covers server-side session loss, logout from another tab, expiry).
  7. Two raw-fetch call sites given the Bearer header too: expense-list.tsx uploadReceipt (multipart) and settings.tsx handleBackup (backup download).
- QA (curl + agent-browser):
  - curl: login returns {user, token(64)}; Bearer-only GET /api/dashboard → 200 with full JSON; Bearer-only /api/auth/me → 200 user; no cookie+no token → 401 (security intact).
  - UI login → token stored, Dashboard loads. **Cleared ALL browser cookies (iframe simulation) → reload → Dashboard STILL LOADS via Bearer (200 in dev.log), "Total Staff" KPIs render, no error state.** Bearer-only POST/DELETE staff-category create→delete → 200. Raw-fetch logout via Bearer → session destroyed; stale token auto-cleared on next boot (verified).
  - Full UI cycle re-verified after fix: login → Dashboard → Users module (New User button) → VAT module (Add Bill button) → UI logout → back to login screen with localStorage token cleared.
  - Console/errors clean after fresh reload (old api-client HMR artifacts gone); tsc 0 errors; lint 0 problems; dev.log shows only 200s post-fix.
- NOTE for the user: the fix requires ONE fresh sign-in from the login screen (so the token gets stored) — then the preview panel keeps them signed in.

Stage Summary:
- Login in the sandbox preview iframe now works end-to-end: the app authenticates via a localStorage Bearer token when the browser blocks the session cookie, while the httpOnly cookie remains the primary mechanism for direct/new-tab access. Logout, 401 handling and stale-token cleanup all covered.
- The user should simply sign in again with admin@landmarkintergulf.com / Landmark@2025 — the dashboard will now load inside the preview panel (and in "Open in New Tab" as before).
- Gotchas for the cron agent: (1) do NOT remove the Bearer fallback in getSessionUser or the Authorization header in api-client — the preview panel depends on it; (2) any NEW raw fetch() call must attach the Bearer header (pattern: getAuthToken() ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${getAuthToken()` } : undefined); (3) /api/auth/me returns 200 with user:null when unauthenticated (not 401) — the 401 auto-clear does not fire for it; boot() handles the stale-token cleanup instead; (4) keep the login response shape {user, token}.

- Settings API: new keys companyNameAr, addressAr, vatNumber, crNumber, bankName, bankIban, bankAccountNumber (GET defaults incl. Arabic defaults, zod-validated PUT). Settings view: Arabic name/address inputs (dir=rtl), "ZATCA e-Invoicing" card (VAT reg no + CR no + Phase 1+2 ready note), "Bank Details" card.

Bugs found & fixed during QA:
1. iban/swiftCode zod preprocessors didn't map undefined→null → 400 "expected string, received undefined" on partial payloads (quick-add). Fixed (…: null).
2. Radix Select fires spurious onValueChange('') when the value's SelectItem isn't mounted (content renders only while open) — this silently reset form.clientId after quick-adding a client. Fixed with an early-return guard on v === '' plus a deterministic SelectValue children render.
3. Old dev server process survived pkill -f "bun run dev" (actual proc is `node next dev` / next-server) → stale Prisma client caused invoice create 500. Fixed kill pattern; also cleared .next once.

- prisma/schema.prisma Invoice model += (all nullable/defaulted, backward compatible): zatcaPhase Int @default(1), zatcaStatus?, zatcaResponse?, zatcaSubmittedAt?, zatcaSubmissionMode?, approvedBy/approvedByTitle/receivedBy/receivedByTitle?, bankName/bankAccountName/bankAccountNumber/bankIban/bankSwiftCode/bankAddress? + @@index([zatcaPhase]) / @@index([zatcaStatus]). `bun run db:push` applied (existing rows got zatcaPhase=1).
- src/app/api/settings/route.ts: added bankAccountName (max 120), bankSwiftCode (max 20), bankAddress (max 240) to GET defaults (''), zod schema and PUT entries. types.ts AppSettings += the 3 fields; store.ts DEFAULT_SETTINGS += '' × 3 (type-compat only, no UI).
- src/lib/zatca.ts major upgrade:
  - ZatcaStampSource += phase: 1 | 2. buildZatcaStamp is now PHASE-AWARE: Phase 1 → QR payload = TLV tags 1-5 ONLY, crypto skipped entirely (no keypair generation, nothing persisted, existing stored stamp ignored but kept); returns phase:1 with uuid/invoiceHash/previousHash/signature/publicKeyBase64 = ''. Phase 2 → unchanged full 8-tag stamp.
  - ZATCA connection profile in the Setting table (zatca_* keys): ZatcaEnv ('simulation'|'preprod'|'production'), ZATCA_ENV_BASES per-env gateway URLs, readZatcaProfile() (never returns the raw CSID token — only csidTokenSet), readZatcaCsidToken(), writeZatcaSetting(), saveZatcaLastTest(), ZatcaProfile/ZatcaLastTest types. Explicit zatca_api_base wins over the env default.
  - hasZatcaKeys() — existence check without generating.
  - computeInvoiceCounter(): ICV reconstructed by walking the PIH chain backwards (single query + in-memory map; robust vs deletions, replaces the old count+1).
  - buildZatcaSubmission(): stamp + UBL XML + icv + restamped flag. Verifies hashInvoiceXml(xml) === stored hash; if drifted (e.g. seller settings changed after stamping) re-hashes + re-signs + persists so printed QR / stored stamp / submitted XML always agree.
  - Invoice-record adapters for the routes: ZatcaInvoiceRecord (Prisma payload type), zatcaInvoiceData() (items sorted by sr), zatcaStampSource(), buildInvoiceStamp(), buildInvoiceSubmission().
  - BUG FIX (pre-existing, found by E2E): fresh stamps used to hash the UBL built with uuid='' (record not yet persisted) so any later rebuild drifted → surprise re-stamp between print and submit. Fresh-stamp XML now embeds the FINAL assigned uuid; buildZatcaSubmission overrides uuid from stamp.uuid (caller record may be stale).
- types.ts: InvoiceRow += zatcaPhase, zatcaStatus, zatcaSubmittedAt, approvedBy/Title, receivedBy/Title, bankName/bankAccountName/bankAccountNumber/bankIban/bankSwiftCode/bankAddress; ZatcaStamp += phase: number.
- /api/invoices (list+create): zod += zatcaPhase (int 1-2, default 1) + optionalText fields ("" → null via z.preprocess) for the 10 signature/bank fields; POST persists them; GET accepts ?zatcaPhase=1|2 and ?zatcaStatus=reported|cleared|rejected; list serialization returns all new fields.
- /api/invoices/[id] (GET/PUT/DELETE): same zod additions; PUT persists new fields; STAMP INVALIDATION — if items/issueDate/client/totals changed AND a stored invoiceHash exists → uuid/invoiceHash/previousHash/signature set null and zatcaStatus reported/cleared reset to null (resubmission needed). Phase 2→1 leaves stored crypto as-is (ignored); 1→2 re-stamps on next fetch. Items include now ordered by sr for deterministic change detection. POST/PUT catch logs console.error for diagnostics.
- /api/invoices/[id]/zatca (existing stamp route): passes phase from invoice.zatcaPhase (2→2 else 1); returns phase in the payload; refactored onto buildInvoiceStamp.
- NEW /api/zatca/profile (GET invoices.view / PUT settings.edit — same permission as the settings route): GET returns { env, egsSerial, csidTokenSet, apiBase, lastTest }; PUT accepts { env?, egsSerial?, csidToken?, apiBase? } — csidToken '' keeps existing / null clears; apiBase ''|null resets to the env default; activity-logged (update/settings).
- NEW /api/zatca/test-connection (POST, invoices.view): simulation env → LOCAL checks only (seller VAT 15 digits, EGS serial "N-value|N-value" format, EC keypair present) + note that no gateway call is made; preprod/production → real GET {apiBase}/invoices/pid with optional Bearer (AbortSignal.timeout(8000)); network failure reported clearly as ok:false (expected offline) never thrown. Result persisted as zatca_last_test either way.
- NEW /api/invoices/[id]/zatca/report (POST, invoices.edit): Phase 2 only (400 "Only Phase 2 invoices can be submitted to ZATCA"), drafts rejected (400 "Issue the invoice first"). Rebuilds the stamp if missing, builds the UBL, runs local validation → checks [{name, ok, severity, detail}]: seller VAT (error), buyer VAT (warning-level — unregistered buyer allowed), totals vatAmount ≈ subtotal×rate ±0.01 (error), items non-empty (error); error-severity failures → 400 with the checks. Simulation mode (env=simulation, or preprod/prod without CSID token → fallback with note): realistic response { status 202/200, REPORTED/CLEARED, reportingTime, uuid, invoiceHash, ICV, clearanceUuid (sim-…, standard only), checks } persisted to zatcaStatus/zatcaSubmittedAt/zatcaSubmissionMode='simulation'/zatcaResponse. Production mode: POST {invoiceHash, uuid, invoice: base64(xml)} to /invoices/report/single (simplified) or /invoices/clearance/single (standard) with Bearer CSID + 15s timeout; maps reportingStatus/clearanceStatus → reported|cleared|rejected; fetch failure → 502 with actionable message. Response: { zatcaStatus, zatcaSubmittedAt, mode, checks, response }. Activity-logged (submitted/invoice).
- NEW /api/invoices/[id]/zatca/ubl (GET, invoices.view): Phase 2 only (400); returns the UBL 2.1 XML as a download (application/xml; charset=utf-8, Content-Disposition attachment; filename="INV-….xml") — byte-identical to the hashed/submitted document.
- Dev server had a STALE PRISMA CLIENT after db:push (known issue from earlier worklogs — POST /api/invoices 500 "Unknown argument clientId", turbopack persistent cache held an ancient DMMF): killed next dev + next-server, cleared .next, restarted with `(setsid nohup bash -c "cd /home/z/my-project && bun run dev 2>&1 | tee -a dev.log" …&)`. Server healthy on port 3000.

Verification (all passed — 78-assertion authenticated E2E via bun script + curl):
- Unauthenticated curls on every new route → 401 (routing works): /api/zatca/profile, /api/zatca/test-connection, /api/invoices/[id]/zatca/report, /api/invoices/[id]/zatca/ubl.
- Profile: defaults env=simulation + per-env apiBase; PUT egsSerial; explicit apiBase override; '' resets to env default; raw CSID token never in any response (csidTokenSet only); '' token keeps existing, null clears.
- test-connection simulation: 3 local checks (VAT/EGS/keypair) + note, zatca_last_test persisted; preprod offline → ok:false with clear error, no crash.
- Phase 1 invoice: zatcaPhase=1, 5-tag TLV QR decoded (tags 1-5 with correct seller/VAT/timestamp/total/VAT), crypto fields '' (no keypair touched), report → 400 "Only Phase 2…", UBL → 400. Signature blocks + per-invoice bank override persisted and serialized in list/detail.
- Phase 2 invoice: 8-tag QR (tag6=hash, tag7=signature, tag8=88-char raw pubkey), stamp stable across refetch; UBL download 200 with correct headers, embeds PIH + stamp uuid; report simulation → standard=cleared (clearanceUuid sim-…), simplified=reported, response carries the invoice hash; unregistered buyer → warning severity, still cleared; invoice row carries zatcaStatus/zatcaSubmittedAt.
- Filters: ?zatcaPhase=2&zatcaStatus=cleared and ?zatcaPhase=1 isolate rows correctly.
- Invalidation: items edit on a stamped+cleared Phase 2 invoice → hash/uuid/signature nulled, zatcaStatus reset; re-stamp produces a different hash; mark-paid quick update still works; 2→1 keeps stored crypto + yields 5-tag QR without deleting it; 1→2 reuses the stored stamp; PIH chain: a new Phase 2 stamp links to the latest stamped invoice.
- Production path in the offline sandbox: 502 with clear error (never a 500 crash); preprod-without-token falls back to simulation with an explanatory note.
- Pre-existing Hyundai invoice (created before this task) still stamps fine as Phase 1 (5-tag QR) — backward compatible.
- bunx tsc --noEmit clean for src (skills/ folder has 2 pre-existing unrelated errors); bun run lint clean; dev.log after restart has zero 500s / zero runtime errors; DB left pristine (test invoices + client deleted; profile restored to simulation with EGS serial demo value; demo bank settings extended with account name/SWIFT/address).

Stage Summary:
- ZATCA Phase 1 (Generation) and Phase 2 (Integration) are now SEPARATE PER-INVOICE designations: zatcaPhase=1 invoices get the 5-tag QR with zero crypto; zatcaPhase=2 invoices get the full 8-tag cryptographic stamp, downloadable UBL 2.1 XML and a submission lifecycle (reported/cleared/rejected) via the new report endpoint.
- Phase 2 "connect system" is configurable and testable: /api/zatca/profile (env simulation/preprod/production, EGS serial, CSID token stored server-side only, editable gateway URL) + /api/zatca/test-connection (local validation in simulation; real gateway probe in preprod/production, offline-safe). Simulation submissions produce realistic persisted gateway responses; production submissions POST the exact UBL to Fatoora report/clearance endpoints.
- Boss signature requirements persisted per invoice: Approved By (+title) and Received By (+title), plus a full per-invoice bank-details override block (null = company Settings fallback). Settings gained bankAccountName/bankSwiftCode/bankAddress.
- Hash-chain integrity hardening: stamp/submit/UBL now guaranteed consistent (fresh-stamp XML embeds the final UUID; drifted stamps self-heal via re-sign; ICV derived from the actual PIH chain). Editing stamped content (items/dates/client/totals) invalidates the stamp and forces re-stamp + resubmission.
- Gotchas for future agents (esp. Task B frontend): (1) InvoiceRow now carries zatcaPhase/zatcaStatus/zatcaSubmittedAt/approvedBy/receivedBy/bank* — the print view should show Approved By (default settings.authorizedSignatory) + Received By lines and prefer invoice bank fields over Settings; (2) ZatcaStamp.phase tells the print view whether to render the crypto block (Phase 2) or just the QR (Phase 1); (3) DEV SERVER MUST BE RESTARTED (and .next cleared) after any prisma db:push — turbopack's persistent cache serves a stale Prisma client otherwise (500 "Unknown argument"); (4) zatca_csid_token Setting is sensitive — never return it from an API; (5) POST /api/invoices and PUT accept zatcaPhase + the 10 optional text fields (empty string → null); (6) list filters ?zatcaPhase= & ?zatcaStatus= available for the UI.

Saves provider results to memory (upsert sourceText+context, usage++). NEVER throws — failures → source 'failed' + single top-level providerError.

════════ API CONTRACT (for the EI-B frontend agent) ════════

Auth: session cookie OR `Authorization: Bearer <token>` (login POST /api/auth/login {email,password} → {user, token}). All routes below need `einvoices.*` / `settings.*` permissions (BASELINE grants all to every signed-in user).

Types (src/lib/types.ts): EInvoiceRow (all scalar fields as ISO strings / numbers / booleans + derived `overdue: boolean`, optional `clientName`, `itemCount`, `items?: EInvoiceItemRow[]`, `payments?: EInvoicePaymentRow[]`, `relatedInvoiceNumber?: string|null`, `arManualFlags: string[]`), EInvoiceItemRow, EInvoicePaymentRow, EInvoiceConfig, GlossaryTermRow. Constants: EINVOICE_DOC_TYPES, EINVOICE_STATUSES, EINVOICE_FILTER_STATUSES (+'overdue').

1) GET /api/e-invoices?search&status&clientId&dateFrom&dateTo&year&page&pageSize (10/25/50/100)
   → { rows: EInvoiceRow[](no items; has itemCount, clientName, relatedInvoiceNumber), totalCount, totalPages, page, pageSize, totals: { count, totalDue, paid, outstanding, overdueCount, overdueAmount }, years: number[] }
   search hits invoiceNumber/clientNameEn/Ar/projectEn/Ar/poRef; status accepts draft|issued|partially_paid|paid|overdue (overdue DERIVED server-side); dateFrom/dateTo on issueDate (win over ?year).

2) POST /api/e-invoices → 201 EInvoiceRow (with items)
   Body: { docType?, clientId?, clientNameEn!, clientNameAr?, clientVatNumber?, clientCrNumber?, clientAddressEn?, clientAddressAr?, poRef?, contractRef?, projectEn/Ar?, paymentTermsEn/Ar?, paymentMethodEn/Ar?, issueDate!(yyyy-mm-dd), supplyDate?, dueDate?, notesEn/Ar?, showReceiverSignature?, arManualFlags?: string[], relatedInvoiceId?, items: [{ descriptionEn!, descriptionAr?, descArManual?, unitEn?, unitAr?, unitArManual?, quantity!(>0), unitPrice!(≥0), discount?(≥0), vatPercent?(0-100, default 15) }] }
   Server: generates invoiceNumber (INV/CRN/DBN-YYYY-00001), status='draft', computes ALL money (never trust client math), copies client.nameAr/addressAr into empty invoice Ar fields, backfills client record from invoice Ar, validates relatedInvoiceId (must exist, not draft) for note types, 400/409 on errors (first zod message).

3) GET /api/e-invoices/next-number?docType=invoice|credit_note|debit_note → { nextNumber, docType } (live form hint; year = current year).

4) GET /api/e-invoices/:id → EInvoiceRow + items (sr asc) + payments (date desc) + client brief + relatedInvoiceNumber. 404 if missing.
   PUT /api/e-invoices/:id (DRAFT ONLY — else 400 "Issued invoices are locked — use a Credit/Debit Note for corrections") — same body as POST; items fully replaced; invoiceNumber unchanged; totals recomputed; returns full row.
   DELETE /api/e-invoices/:id (DRAFT ONLY) → { deleted: true, id }.

5) POST /api/e-invoices/:id/issue (draft only) → { ...EInvoiceRow, qrPayload, qrDataUrl }
   Assigns uuid (v4), icv (chain counter), previousHash (last issued invoiceHash ?? ZATCA_INITIAL_PIH), invoiceHash (b64 SHA-256 of UBL XML), status 'issued', issuedAt, issuedByName. Print-ready QR included in response.

6) POST /api/e-invoices/:id/duplicate (any status) → 201 new DRAFT row (new number, same docType, items + Ar/manual flags + header copied; payments/uuid/icv/hashes/signedCopy NOT copied; relatedInvoiceId kept only for note types).

7) POST /api/e-invoices/:id/payments (issued/partially_paid only; draft → 400 "Issue the invoice first"; paid → 400) — body { amount!(>0), paymentDate!, methodEn?, methodAr?, reference?, notes? } → 201 { row (full row incl. payments), payment: EInvoicePaymentRow, paidAmount, status } (paidAmount ≥ totalDue−0.005 → 'paid' else 'partially_paid').
   DELETE /api/e-invoices/:id/payments/:paymentId → { row, paidAmount, status, deletedPaymentId } (no payments left → back to 'issued').

8) GET /api/e-invoices/:id/qr (works for DRAFTS too — tag 3 uses issueDate) → { qrPayload, qrDataUrl } (600×600 PNG, quiet zone 4, ECC M, black/white).

9) PUT /api/e-invoices/:id/signed-copy — body { image: dataUrl } (PNG/JPEG ≤2MB magic-byte validated; WORKS ON ISSUED/LOCKED invoices — proof of delivery does not unlock editing) → { signedCopyImage }. DELETE → { signedCopyImage: null }.

10) POST /api/e-invoices/translate (permission einvoices.create OR einvoices.edit) — body { entries: [{ key!, text!(≤2000), context? }] } max 60 → ALWAYS 200: { results: [{ key, translated, source: 'glossary'|'memory'|'provider'|'skipped'|'failed' }], providerError?: string }. skipped → leave the English as-is (numbers/refs/already-Arabic); failed → warn + let user type Arabic manually (translated=''). Contexts: client_name|address|description|unit|project|payment_terms|payment_method|notes|bank_name|generic. Call this BEFORE saving; apply results to fields NOT flagged manual (header arManualFlags keys: clientName|clientAddress|project|paymentTerms|paymentMethod|notes; per-item descArManual/unitArManual).

11) GET /api/e-invoice-config (settings.view) → { letterhead(dataUrl|''), topMm, bottomMm, signature, stamp, showReceiverSignature, translateProvider: 'builtin'|'google'|'deepl', translateApiKeySet: boolean } — the API key is NEVER returned.
    PUT (settings.edit) — any subset: { letterhead?: dataUrl|null (null/'' clears), topMm?: 0-100, bottomMm?: 0-100, signature?, stamp?, showReceiverSignature?: boolean, translateProvider?, translateApiKey?: string|null (''/null clears, undefined keeps) } → returns the fresh GET shape.

12) GET /api/glossary (settings.view) → { data: GlossaryTermRow[] } (termEn asc). POST (settings.edit) { termEn!, termAr!, notes? } → 201 row | 409 duplicate. PUT /api/glossary/:id { termEn?, termAr?, notes? } → row. DELETE /api/glossary/:id → { deleted: true }.

Frontend notes/gotchas:
- src/lib/e-invoice.ts + src/lib/translate.ts are SERVER-ONLY (Prisma import) — do NOT import from client components (duplicate the tiny compute helpers client-side for live previews; the server recomputes on save anyway). src/lib/arabic-words.ts IS client-safe: amountToWordsArabic(amount, currency) + amountInWordsBoth(amount, currency) → { en, ar } for the bilingual amount-in-words line.
- arManualFlags API contract: send/expect a plain JSON ARRAY of header keys (server stores it as a JSON string internally).
- The translate route never 500s — degrade gracefully per entry.
- E2E re-run anytime: `bun run scripts/ei-e2e.ts` (95 assertions; needs dev server up) then `bun run scripts/ei-cleanup.ts` to wipe ALL e-invoices/glossary/memory + reset einvoice settings (issued test rows can't be API-deleted by design — the cleanup script goes through Prisma directly).
- After ANY future `bun run db:push`: restart dev server + `rm -rf .next` (turbopack serves a stale Prisma client otherwise).
- Login for QA/admin: admin@landmarkintergulf.com / Landmark@2025.

- Rewrote pagination in src/components/app/print/quotation-print.tsx:

- Continuation strip now renders on EVERY page ≥ 2 (was items-only): logo (none mode) + "Company — Quotation QTN-xxxx · Client" + gold CONTINUED chip.
- Signatures section keeps mt-auto (pins to page foot — standard business look).

Task ID: FIX-AI-LOGS-DUPKEY
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: User-reported console error — "Encountered two children with the same key, `site_manpower`" in Settings > AI Assistant request-log table (src/components/app/settings/ai-settings.tsx:481).

Work Log:
- Root cause: AiUsageLog.toolsCalled is a JSON array of tool names per request; when the agent calls the same tool twice in one request (e.g. ["site_manpower","search_employees","site_manpower"] in log cmvh7oiws…), rendering one Badge per entry used the tool name as React key → duplicate keys.
- Fix in src/components/app/settings/ai-settings.tsx: added aggregateTools() helper (Map-based name→count aggregation over parseTools()); logs table now renders one Badge per UNIQUE tool with a muted "×N" suffix when called more than once; key={t.name} is guaranteed unique.
- Verified with agent-browser: navigated Dashboard → Settings → AI Assistant section; DB-confirmed the offending log row (site_manpower ×2) still present and rendered as "site_manpower ×2 search_employees" badges; console clean (0 errors/warnings); `bun run lint` clean.

- Backend: new route POST /api/name-lists/quick-worker (src/app/api/name-lists/quick-worker/route.ts). Body = quick worker fields + optional listId. With listId → creates the Staff record AND adds it to that list in ONE request (interview-kind lists set deploymentStatus=in_interview, appended with sortOrder at end, read-only lists rejected). Without listId → standalone create (used by the New-List dialog's draft selection). Iqama is OPTIONAL: blank → TEMP-###### placeholder (Staff.iqamaId is NOT NULL+UNIQUE), response carries tempIqama flag so the UI warns "update the real number later from Employees". Duplicate-Iqama → 409 with the existing worker's name. Blood group whitelisted. Activity logs: staff 'created' (notes quick-add origin + list no) + namelist 'updated'. Permission: namelist.edit (module-scoped action by design).
- Frontend: new QuickAddWorkerDialog (views/name-list/quick-add-worker.tsx) — Name* autoFocus (Enter submits), Trade select (auto-fetched categories or passed in), Iqama/Passport/Mobile/Nationality (datalist = common Saudi workforce nationalities + existing DB ones), Blood Group select, Experience years; mobile preview hint; disabled until name typed.
